EXISTS ../tokkl/packages/api/src/models/timeEntries.ts True
function snapToResolution(db: Db, user: UserRow, start: Date, stop: Date): { start: Date; stop: Date } {
  return snapSpan(wholeSeconds(start), wholeSeconds(stop), getTimeResolutionMinutes(db), user.timezone);
}

function readTagIds(db: Db, wid: number, body: Record<string, unknown>): number[] | undefined {
  let ids: number[] | undefined;
  if (body.tag_ids !== undefined && body.tag_ids !== null) {
    if (!Array.isArray(body.tag_ids)) throw badRequest('Invalid tag_ids: expected an array');
    ids = body.tag_ids.map((value) => {
      const id = optionalId(value, 'tag_ids');
      if (!id) throw badRequest('Invalid tag_ids');
      return getTag(db, wid, id).id;
    });
  }
  if (body.tags !== undefined && body.tags !== null) {
    if (!Array.isArray(body.tags) || body.tags.some((tag) => typeof tag !== 'string')) {
      throw badRequest('Invalid tags: expected an array of strings');
    }
    ids = [...(ids ?? []), ...resolveTagNames(db, wid, body.tags as string[])];
  }
  return ids === undefined ? undefined : [...new Set(ids)];
}

function applyTagAction(current: number[], incoming: number[] | undefined, action: unknown): number[] {
  if (incoming === undefined) return current;
  if (action === 'add') return [...new Set([...current, ...incoming])];
  if (action === 'delete' || action === 'remove') return current.filter((id) => !incoming.includes(id));
  return incoming;
}

function readDuration(value: unknown): number | undefined {
  if (value === undefined || value === null) return undefined;
  if (typeof value !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(value)) throw badRequest('Invalid duration: expected an integer');
  return Math.trunc(value);
}

function readStart(body: Record<string, unknown>, fallback: Date): Date {
  const value = body.start ?? fallback.toISOString();
  const start = parseInstant(value, 'start');
  if (body.start_date === undefined || body.start_date === null) return start;
  if (typeof body.start_date !== 'string' || !parseDateKey(body.start_date)) {
    throw badRequest('Invalid start_date: expected YYYY-MM-DD');
  }
  return parseInstant(`${body.start_date}${(value as string).slice(10)}`, 'start');
}

function assertConsistentDuration(start: Date, stop: Date | null, duration: number | undefined, body: Record<string, unknown>): void {
  if (body.stop != null && stop && duration !== undefined && seconds(stop) - seconds(start) !== duration) {
    throw badRequest('Stop and duration must be consistent with start');
  }
}

package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.credentials

import android.content.Context
import android.security.keystore.KeyGenParameterSpec
import android.security.keystore.KeyProperties
import android.util.AtomicFile
import java.io.File
import java.io.FileNotFoundException
import java.io.IOException
import java.security.GeneralSecurityException
import java.security.KeyStore
import javax.crypto.Cipher
import javax.crypto.KeyGenerator
import javax.crypto.SecretKey
import javax.crypto.spec.GCMParameterSpec

/**
 * Keeps the API token encrypted with an AES-GCM key in the Android Keystore
 * (docs/PRD.md S5). The key never leaves the Keystore. The app-private file
 * holds the IV and the ciphertext, never the plain token.
 *
 * The store has no backup rules of its own: the app keeps all of its data out
 * of backup and device transfer, and another phone could not decrypt the file
 * anyway.
 *
 * Calls do disk and Keystore I/O, so make them off the main thread.
 */
[credential-related line omitted]

    private val file = AtomicFile(File(context.filesDir, FILE_NAME))

    /** Encrypts [token] and replaces the stored one. */
    @Throws(GeneralSecurityException::class, IOException::class)
    fun save(token: String) {
        synchronized(LOCK) {
            val cipher = Cipher.getInstance(TRANSFORMATION)
            // The Keystore picks a random IV for every encryption.
            cipher.init(Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, key() ?: createKey())
            val iv = cipher.iv
            val ciphertext = cipher.doFinal(token.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8))
            write(byteArrayOf(FORMAT_VERSION, iv.size.toByte()) + iv + ciphertext)
        }
    }

    /**
     * The stored token, or null when there is none or it no longer decrypts,
     * for example because the key is gone or the file was changed. Either way
     * the user has to sign in again.
     */
    @Throws(GeneralSecurityException::class, IOException::class)
    fun load(): String? {
        synchronized(LOCK) {
            val bytes = try {
                file.readFully()
            } catch (e: FileNotFoundException) {
                return null
            }
            if (bytes.size < HEADER_SIZE || bytes[0] != FORMAT_VERSION) return null
            val ivEnd = HEADER_SIZE + (bytes[1].toInt() and 0xFF)
            if (bytes.size < ivEnd + TAG_BITS / 8) return null
            val key = key() ?: return null
            return try {
                val cipher = Cipher.getInstance(TRANSFORMATION)
                cipher.init(Cipher.DECRYPT_MODE, key, GCMParameterSpec(TAG_BITS, bytes, HEADER_SIZE, ivEnd - HEADER_SIZE))
                String(cipher.doFinal(bytes, ivEnd, bytes.size - ivEnd), Charsets.UTF_8)
            } catch (e: GeneralSecurityException) {
                null
            }
        }
    }

    /** Deletes the ciphertext and the Keystore key. Signing out calls this (TKA-0038). */
    @Throws(GeneralSecurityException::class)
    fun clear() {
        synchronized(LOCK) {
            file.delete()
            keyStore().deleteEntry(KEY_ALIAS)
        }
    }

    private fun write(bytes: ByteArray) {
        val out = file.startWrite()
        try {
            out.write(bytes)
            file.finishWrite(out)
        } catch (e: IOException) {
            file.failWrite(out)
            throw e
        }
    }

    private fun key(): SecretKey? = keyStore().getKey(KEY_ALIAS, null) as SecretKey?

    /** Keystore keys cannot be exported: only the Keystore ever sees the key material. */
    private fun createKey(): SecretKey {
        val spec = KeyGenParameterSpec.Builder(KEY_ALIAS, KeyProperties.PURPOSE_ENCRYPT or KeyProperties.PURPOSE_DECRYPT)
            .setKeySize(KEY_BITS)
            .setBlockModes(KeyProperties.BLOCK_MODE_GCM)
            .setEncryptionPaddings(KeyProperties.ENCRYPTION_PADDING_NONE)
            .build()
        return KeyGenerator.getInstance(KeyProperties.KEY_ALGORITHM_AES, ANDROID_KEY_STORE)
            .apply { init(spec) }
            .generateKey()
    }

    private fun keyStore(): KeyStore = KeyStore.getInstance(ANDROID_KEY_STORE).apply { load(null) }

    internal companion object {
[credential-related line omitted]
[credential-related line omitted]
        const val ANDROID_KEY_STORE = "AndroidKeyStore"

        private const val TRANSFORMATION = "AES/GCM/NoPadding"
        private const val KEY_BITS = 256
        private const val TAG_BITS = 128

        /** The file is the format version, the IV length, the IV and the ciphertext with its GCM tag. */
        private const val FORMAT_VERSION: Byte = 1
        private const val HEADER_SIZE = 2

        /** One lock for every instance, since they share the file and the key. */
        private val LOCK = Any()
    }
}

app/src/main/java/com/github/e0ipso/tokkl/time/JsSemantics.kt
package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.time

import kotlin.math.floor

// Helpers that give the ports of Tokkl's TypeScript the JavaScript behaviour where Kotlin's differs.

/**
 * A regex class of the characters JavaScript's `\s` and `String.prototype.trim` treat as white
 * space: ECMAScript WhiteSpace and LineTerminator. Java's `\s` matches only ASCII white space, and
 * Kotlin's `trim()` uses yet another set.
 */
internal const val JS_WHITESPACE =
    """[\t\n\u000B\f\r \u00A0\u1680\u2000-\u200A\u2028\u2029\u202F\u205F\u3000\uFEFF]"""

/** JavaScript's `/\s+/g`. */
internal val JS_WHITESPACE_RUN = Regex("$JS_WHITESPACE+")

private val JS_TRIM = Regex("""^$JS_WHITESPACE+|$JS_WHITESPACE+\z""")

/** JavaScript's `String.prototype.trim`. */
internal fun String.jsTrim(): String = replace(JS_TRIM, "")

/** JavaScript's `Math.round`, which rounds halves up. [kotlin.math.round] rounds them to even. */
internal fun jsRound(x: Double): Double {
    if (!x.isFinite()) return x
    val whole = floor(x)
    return if (x - whole >= 0.5) whole + 1 else whole
}

---
name: verification-before-completion
description: Use when about to claim work is complete, fixed, or passing, before committing or creating PRs - requires running verification commands and confirming output before making any success claims; evidence before assertions always
---

# Verification Before Completion

## Overview

**Core principle:** Evidence before claims, always.

**Violating the letter of this rule is violating the spirit of this rule.**

## The Iron Law

```
NO COMPLETION CLAIMS WITHOUT FRESH VERIFICATION EVIDENCE
```

If you haven't run the verification command in this message, you cannot claim it passes.

## The Gate Function

```
BEFORE claiming any status or expressing satisfaction:

1. IDENTIFY: What command proves this claim?
2. RUN: Execute the FULL command (fresh, complete)
3. READ: Full output, check exit code, count failures
4. VERIFY: Does output confirm the claim?
   - If NO: State actual status with evidence
   - If YES: State claim WITH evidence
5. ONLY THEN: Make the claim

Skip any step = lying, not verifying
```

## Common Failures

| Claim | Requires | Not Sufficient |
|-------|----------|----------------|
| Tests pass | Test command output: 0 failures | Previous run, "should pass" |
| Linter clean | Linter output: 0 errors | Partial check, extrapolation |
| Build succeeds | Build command: exit 0 | Linter passing, logs look good |
| Bug fixed | Test original symptom: passes | Code changed, assumed fixed |
| Regression test works | Red-green cycle verified | Test passes once |
| Agent completed | VCS diff shows changes | Agent reports "success" |
| Requirements met | Line-by-line checklist | Tests passing |

## Red Flags - STOP

- Using "should", "probably", "seems to"
- Expressing satisfaction before verification ("Great!", "Perfect!", "Done!", etc.)
- About to commit/push/PR without verification
- Trusting agent success reports
- Relying on partial verification
- Thinking "just this once"
- Tired and wanting work over
- **ANY wording implying success without having run verification**

## Rationalization Prevention

| Excuse | Reality |
|--------|---------|
| "Should work now" | RUN the verification |
| "I'm confident" | Confidence ≠ evidence |
| "Just this once" | No exceptions |
| "Linter passed" | Linter ≠ compiler |
| "Agent said success" | Verify independently |
| "I'm tired" | Exhaustion ≠ excuse |
| "Partial check is enough" | Partial proves nothing |
| "Different words so rule doesn't apply" | Spirit over letter |

## Key Patterns

**Tests:**
```
✅ [Run test command] [See: 34/34 pass] "All tests pass"
❌ "Should pass now" / "Looks correct"
```

**Regression tests (TDD Red-Green):**
```
✅ Write → Run (pass) → Revert fix → Run (MUST FAIL) → Restore → Run (pass)
❌ "I've written a regression test" (without red-green verification)
```

**Build:**
```
✅ [Run build] [See: exit 0] "Build passes"
❌ "Linter passed" (linter doesn't check compilation)
```

**Requirements:**
```
✅ Re-read plan → Create checklist → Verify each → Report gaps or completion
❌ "Tests pass, phase complete"
```

**Agent delegation:**
```
✅ Agent reports success → Check VCS diff → Verify changes → Report actual state
❌ Trust agent report
```

## When To Apply

**ALWAYS before:**
- ANY variation of success/completion claims
- ANY expression of satisfaction
- ANY positive statement about work state
- Committing, PR creation, task completion
- Moving to next task
- Delegating to agents

**Rule applies to:**
- Exact phrases
- Paraphrases and synonyms
- Implications of success
- ANY communication suggesting completion/correctness

  confidence: high
  category: requirement
  ticket: TKA-0016
  location: app/src/main/java/com/github/e0ipso/tokkl/signin/SignInModel.kt:57
  problem: Recreating the app process clears the 429 cooldown before Retry-After expires.
  evidence: After a 429 with Retry-After: 60, terminate and reopen the app within 60 seconds; the new model initializes retryAt to null, allowing another request after credentials are re-entered.
